    93	## Applicable design
    94	
    95	`$HOME/Workspace/dotfiles/.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md` section 5.14 applies **by reference**, including items 1–10 and the same-UID threat model. Under C-88, the following verbatim sentences are normative; the design path supplies context rather than replacing these requirements.
    96	
    97	Item 2: “A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.” “Python は `-I`。” “PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。”
    98	
    99	Item 4: “検証した実行体はその realpath で実行する（再探索しない）。” “検証したファイル内容は信頼ルート配下のスナップショット（0600、一意名）として渡し、使用直前に sha256 を再照合する。” “判断に読んだ証跡は sha256 を出力に記録する。”
   100	
   101	Item 9: “orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。” “`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。” “One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19).”
   102	
   103	## Item 1 — one trusted interpreter resolver
   104	
   105	Add executable `scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, with English shdoc comments and `#!/bin/bash -p`. Every caller executes it as a program through a trusted parent supplying `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …`; do not source it or use an unqualified `bash <file>` invocation. The parent itself must already be trusted and clean before starting `/usr/bin/env`; clearing the environment after a loader has consumed it is too late. There is no self re-exec.
   106	
   107	Before traps, tool discovery or declaration reads, verify the actual environment's exact allowed names through `/usr/bin/env` and `/usr/bin/grep`, the fixed PATH and LANG values, the script's absolute realpath and the passwd-home match. Account explicitly for Bash's own maintained environment entries so the clean prefix succeeds; allow no sanitization sentinel or production test exemption. Require `$0` to be the bound absolute canonical script pathname and its realpath to be a regular file under the trusted repository root, outside worker roots; refuse a symlinked `$0`, including a worker-owned symlink to the trusted script, without reopening it. Any invalid environment, script path or HOME exits 2 with a named `trusted-interpreter:` diagnostic and no stdout, instead of repairing or re-executing.
   108	
   109	Read only the repository’s exact Python declaration established by the dispatch prerequisite, anchored to the trusted script/repository location rather than the caller’s cwd. Parse its fixed grammar as data with trusted absolute tools, without `source`, `eval`, shell interpolation of declaration content, or an interpreter selected from that content. Refuse missing, duplicate, malformed, non-exact or metacharacter-bearing declarations; `latest` is invalid.
   110	
   111	Obtain the real uid through `/usr/bin/id -ru`. On macOS obtain its user name through `/usr/bin/id -run` and its passwd home through `/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory`; on Linux use `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>`. Refuse a missing, ambiguous or malformed passwd result, or any disagreement between that passwd home and HOME, with exit 2 and a named `trusted-interpreter:` diagnostic before selecting a candidate. Derive the installation root only as `<passwd home>/.local/share/mise/installs`, never from the HOME variable, and construct only `<install root>/python/<version>/bin/python3`. Resolve and validate the candidate before printing it. Require an executable regular file after `realpath`, within the expected protected installation, outside the worktree, manifest worker roots, worker-writable caches and `$TMPDIR`. Check resolved path boundaries, including filesystem case behavior where applicable. Refuse a symlinked installation rather than accepting a redirected install directory.
   112	
   113	On success print exactly one absolute interpreter realpath and exit 0. On every refusal print a named `trusted-interpreter:` diagnostic to stderr, print no interpreter on stdout, and exit 2. There is no `command -v`, `mise which`, PATH search, version-alias selection, download or fallback. Do not execute a candidate to discover whether it is trustworthy.
   114	
   115	This is the common resolver required by item 9. This PR migrates its first consumer only; it does not authorize edits to other orchestrator scripts.
   116

Invoke the resolver by its bound absolute realpath through the same clean prefix, supplying HOME from the verified passwd home, and consume its single validated result; propagate refusal without trying another interpreter.

---
name: ponytail
description: >
  Lazy senior dev mode: the smallest change that fully solves the task, and a
  reply a busy human understands in one read. Use on any coding task (writing,
  fixing, refactoring, reviewing, choosing dependencies) and when the user says
  "ponytail", "be lazy", "simplest solution", "yagni", or complains about
  over-engineering or bloat. Levels: lite, full (default), ultra.
argument-hint: "[lite|full|ultra|off]"
license: MIT
---

# Ponytail

You are a lazy senior developer. The best code is the code never written. You solve the whole problem with the least new code. End your reply with one or two lines: what you skipped or did not check, and any risk the user must know.

Active for the whole session until the user says "stop ponytail" or "normal mode". Switch level: `/ponytail lite|full|ultra`.

## Before you write

Read the task and the code it touches. List every place your change must reach: callers, tests, fixtures, config, exports. Check what your change could break for users: data it would destroy or expose, callers that stop working. That is scope. Extra features are not.

## The smallest complete change

Take the first option that fully works:

1. Does it need to exist? Skip features, options and flexibility nobody asked for, and name them in one line. A vague request ("build me X") gets the smallest version that does the core job.
2. Already in this codebase (a helper, component, service, pattern)? Use it the way the surrounding code does.
3. Standard library or a platform feature? Use it, unless the project has its own. A house component beats a native widget.
4. An installed dependency? Use it. Never add a dependency for a few lines.
5. Can it be one line a reader gets at a glance? One line.
6. Otherwise: the minimum code that works.

- Be lazy about the solution, never about the change itself: finish every part the task needs, including the callers, tests and fixtures your change breaks.
- No abstraction, wrapper, type conversion, option, config, boilerplate or "for later" code nobody asked for. Keep values in the form the platform already gives you. Deletion beats addition. Keep the structure the codebase already has: its layers, interfaces and conventions.
- The shortest working diff wins, once you know everything it must touch. A one-liner that needs decoding is not short.
- Comment only the why the code cannot show, in one line.
- Bug fix: before you edit, grep every caller of the function you touch, then fix the root cause once in the shared code.
- Code you move or merge keeps its error handling and validation.
- Between options of equal size, take the one that is correct on edge cases.
- Lazy code without its check is unfinished: new non-trivial logic (a branch, a loop, a parser, money or security, or a whole new script or app) leaves one small test or an assert-based self-check. Trivial changes need none.
- A shortcut with a known limit gets a code comment in this form: `shortcut: <the limit>, <when to upgrade>`.

Never cut: validation at trust boundaries, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, the calibration real hardware needs, anything the user asked for.

## Levels

| Level | Behavior |
|-------|----------|
| **lite** | Build what was asked. Name the smaller option in one line and let the user pick. |
| **full** | The rules above. Default. |
| **ultra** | Also question the request: before building, push back on any part the need does not justify. |

**Organizing citation paths**

     1	Found one security issue in [PR #424](https://github.com/mryfmo/dotfiles/pull/424), head `44cb835`.
     2	
     3	- **P1 · High confidence · `scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh:45` — The fallback still accepts a checkout-controlled interpreter from an activated virtualenv’s absolute PATH entry, leaving the owned interpreter-selection vulnerability open.**
     4	
     5	  When `mise` is absent or cannot resolve `python3`, `command -v` can return `<checkout>/.venv/bin/python3`. Changing directory to `/` does not neutralize absolute PATH entries, and line 47 accepts this path because its directory is not named `shims`. The selected executable subsequently runs through uv and directly at line 162 with the operator’s authority; the latter retains the inherited environment, including any credentials.
     6	
     7	  Resolve the fallback from explicitly trusted tool locations. Extend the regression test at `tests/unit/test_ci_rerun.py:273` with a marker-writing `python3` in `.venv/bin` **prepended to PATH**, with mise unavailable. The current fixture sets `VIRTUAL_ENV` but omits that activation behavior. This finding is based on code tracing, not an executed exploit.
     8	
     9	Threat-table assessment:
    10	
    11	| Rows | Assessment |
    12	|---|---|
    13	| Script and tests | Changes fit the owned interpreter-selection work, but the finding prevents closure. |
    14	| Workflow/interpreter/environment inputs | **Insufficient mitigation:** excluding interpreter files from `allowed_files` does not prevent the helper from executing checkout-controlled files supplied through PATH. |
    15	| Lint baseline | Unchanged; no baseline suppression introduced. |
    16	| Task copy, metadata, separators | Local SHA-256 matches the report; correspondence with the original dispatch remains unverified. |
    17	| Evidence records | Treated as untrusted claims; historical review receipts do not establish final-head correctness. |
    18	| In-process effects | Test code installs a failing mise stub and fake gh; no live GitHub action is apparent in the fixtures. |
    19	| New/renamed scripts | None introduced. |
    20	| Merge, update-branch, forced removal | Procedural controls remain the orchestrator’s responsibility; this diff neither implements nor verifies their execution. |
    21	
    22	No additional changeset-specific injection, unsafe deserialization, path-traversal, or authentication/authorization issue was identified. YAML continues to use `safe_load`; the earlier pre-pinning `env` and cleanup-command issues are fixed.
    23	
    24	📝 まとめ: Pinned-head security review completed with one P1 finding; acceptance remains with the orchestrator.
    25	Not checked: runtime tests, live CI, or dispatch provenance; filesystem access was read-only and the gh-first API request failed. The remaining risk is checkout-controlled execution with operator authority.
    26	Verdict: incorrect     1	---
     2	task: dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a02
     3	head: 7329f0eee8f2780c254cd7283209eddb92cacc07
     4	pr: 424
     5	base: b22a5580d0778e57a66d3f75c76e3c55b88701e1
     6	started: 2026-10-19T04:44:02Z
     7	finished: 2026-10-19T04:46:53Z
     8	findings: 1
     9	engine: codex review --base main (profile review, read-only)
    10	---
    11	
    12	- [P1] Use an absolute path for cleanup before PATH is pinned — scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh:42-42
    13	
    14	```text
    15	The new cleanup trap introduces an arbitrary-code-execution path when tool resolution fails before PATH is pinned. Syntax checks passed; filesystem-writing tests were not run in the read-only sandbox.
    16	
    17	Review comment:
    18	
    19	- [P1] Use an absolute path for cleanup before PATH is pinned — scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh:42-42
    20	  When invoked from a PR checkout with an empty or `.` PATH entry, a missing or rejected tool causes the loop to exit before PATH is pinned. This trap then resolves `rm` through the inherited PATH, executing a checkout-supplied executable with the operator's privileges and credential access instead of failing closed. Use `/bin/rm` in the trap and extend the planted-tools test to cover tool-resolution failure.
    21	The new cleanup trap introduces an arbitrary-code-execution path when tool resolution fails before PATH is pinned. Syntax checks passed; filesystem-writing tests were not run in the read-only sandbox.
    22	
    23	Review comment:
    24	
    25	- [P1] Use an absolute path for cleanup before PATH is pinned — scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh:42-42
    26	  When invoked from a PR checkout with an empty or `.` PATH entry, a missing or rejected tool causes the loop to exit before PATH is pinned. This trap then resolves `rm` through the inherited PATH, executing a checkout-supplied executable with the operator's privileges and credential access instead of failing closed. 103:1. **書き込み境界（前提 6、ランタイム別に正直に）** Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。worker 設定：除外コマンド無し、Herdr socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。chezmoi は user ではこのファイルを書けないので、描画した内容を操作者が `sudo` で一度配置する（操作者手順）。worker 設定は worktree 自身の `.claude/**` と `.mcp.json` への Edit deny（`//<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**`）を持つ（Bash 側は sandbox 既定の settings/hooks の denyWithinAllow）。agmsg store（全席が書く）から配送 hook が driver や plugin を source する上流の経路（storage.sh の `drivers.partition` traversal、`db/trusted-plugins` と `plugins/partition/shared.sh` による差し替え承認）は別タスク（agmsg hardening）の行で、worker 設定は `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` と `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins` への書き込みを deny し（Edit deny は sandbox の deny にも合流する）、hook 側は store 由来の設定値を検証するまで残余と書く。managed settings の配置先は OS で異なる（macOS `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、Linux `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`）。hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。公式文書：linked worktree の session は **main checkout の `.claude/settings.local.json` を読む**ので、worker 向けの hook を worktree の settings.local.json に書いても読まれず、逆に orchestrator の local hooks が worker session でも走る。したがって orchestrator 側の local hooks も guard 経由にし、worker 側の hook は worker config dir の settings に置く。Edit deny の一覧は `git ls-tree` ではなく描画時の静的一覧（manifest に列挙、`.agents/worklog/**` と `.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**` を含む）とし、`~/.agents/**` の全面 deny は agmsg store の書き込みと矛盾するので、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`、`~/.agents/model-profiles.env` などストア以外を列挙する。公式文書で確認した事実（2026-10-19）：`Edit(path)` の deny は Write/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit にも効き、deny → ask → allow の順で最初の一致が決まる；ただし Read/Edit の deny は **Bash sandbox の設定にも合流する**（"Paths and domains from both sandbox settings and permission rules are merged into the final sandbox configuration"）ので、ユーザー設定（全席共有）に `.orchestration/**` の Edit deny を書くと orchestrator の Bash による記録書き込みも止まる。hook は起動時に固定されず、設定ファイルの編集は file watcher が即座に反映する。`sandbox.failIfUnavailable` の既定は fail-open（依存が無ければ sandbox 無しで実行）。したがって Claude 席の境界は Edit deny 規則ではなく、**ユーザーレベルの PreToolUse hook**（`~/.claude/hooks/worker-write-boundary`、chezmoi が描画、`/bin/sh` または絶対パスの `python3 -I`）で作る：cwd の realpath が `<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/` 配下の session では、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit の対象が cwd の外、または `<cwd>/.claude/**`・`<cwd>/.mcp.json` のとき `permissionDecision: deny` を返す。Bash 側は sandbox の既定 `denyWithinAllow`（settings、hooks）と `allowWrite`（自席 worktree）が担う。`failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。 Codex 席は sandbox（workspace-write）がツールの書き込み全部を自席の worktree と列挙パスに限る。Claude 席は Seatbelt sandbox が Bash を限るだけで、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit、hook、MCP プロセスは sandbox 外で動き、permission policy（permgate の決定論ポリシー）が path で拒否しない限り anchored checkout や `~/.agents` にも書ける（2026-10-19 時点、描画済み設定にその deny は無い）。したがって前提 6 は「Codex 席は成立、Claude 席は P1-15 が描画設定に Edit の path deny を入れるまで残余」と書く。P1-15 以前に「worker は信頼ルートに書けない」と書く文は誤りで、C-80 が拒否する。P1-15 が入るまで、承認の出所は項目 8 の「コミット済み台帳」で守る。書き込みは自席の worktree（`.claude/worktrees/<name>`）、scratch、列挙されたツールキャッシュに限る。anchored checkout（`.claude/worktrees/` 以外）、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.claude/hooks`、信頼ルートのバイナリ、orchestrator の証跡・スナップショット・lock は worker から書けない。render テストが描画後の deny を検証する。残余は「どの席 sandbox にも属さない同一 UID プロセス」と、Worker Playbook step 4 が sandbox 外に出す少数のコマンド（gh）。後者は D-024-17（P1-15）で絶対パスの実行体に束縛する。
104:2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
109:0. **脅威モデル（D-024-21）** 席は同じ OS ユーザで動き、ホストは席間の信頼境界ではない（同一 UID のプロセスは symlink 差し替え、共有 `.git` の object 差し替え、hook の子プロセス、Herdr socket 経由の host 実行で、ホスト内のどの検査も迂回できる）。regime の硬い境界は GitHub（保護された main、必須チェック、orchestrator の identity によるマージ）。ただしこの境界は **worker 席が orchestrator の GitHub 資格情報を共有しない**ときだけ成立する（D-024-22：worker 席は専用の GitHub identity（別アカウントまたは App のトークン）で `gh` に login し、ruleset の bypass 一覧に入らない。orchestrator の keychain トークンを worker の config dir に持ち込まない。identity が分かれるまで「GitHub が境界」は未成立と書く）。**同一 macOS ユーザでは資格情報は分離できない**（gh は keychain の項目を host/user で引き、同じ UID の gh は orchestrator のトークンを読める。`GH_CONFIG_DIR` は設定を分けるだけ）。防止には worker 席を別の OS ユーザ（または別ホスト）で動かすしかなく、それは D-024-23 の将来項目（P1-16、操作者の判断）。それまで GitHub 境界は **検知**で守る：記録は boundary の記録 PR まで main に無いので、検知は次の記録 PR の regime ジョブで行う（`.orchestration/acceptance/**` に触れる PR で、前回 boundary 以降の main の各マージ commit に、gate 通過の証跡を持つ受け入れ記録が対応することを検査し、無ければその PR を失敗させる；P1-11 owner a02 の行 `ci:detection:1`）。main push 時点の検知は記録が無いので不可能であり、文は「次の記録 PR で検知、防止ではない」と書く。悪意ある worker に耐えるべき入力（承認台帳、gate が読む証跡 of record）は GitHub から sha 指定で API 経由（sandbox 内）に読み、ローカルファイルやローカル object database からは読まない。ホスト内の制御（本節 1〜10）は事故と prompt injection による損害の抑制であり、文はそう書く。
110:8. **承認はコミット済み台帳から読む** permission の決定行（`scope=permission approved_by=user diff_sha256=…`）は anchored checkout の作業コピーではなく、コミット済みの台帳から読む。読み方は `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` が返す commit sha を取り、GitHub API（`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/contents/.orchestration/decisions.tsv?ref=<sha>`、sandbox 内）でその sha の内容を取得する。ローカルの `git show` は共有 object database が書けるため中間 tree の差し替えで偽物を返しうる（第 3 回審査の所見 2）（ローカルの ref 名 `origin/main` は経由しない。`refs/replace/` も書けるので replacement を無効にする。Codex worker は `.git/objects` と `.git/refs` に書けるので ref は差し替えられるが、sha で指した object は内容アドレスで偽造できない）。作業コピーの行は提案で、記録 PR が main にマージされて初めて承認になる（PR の承認は操作者の操作）。偽造には main へのマージが要る。 The same applies to the PR content a mechanism evaluates (the verdict, the audit precheck, the counters): the changeset is read from GitHub at the 40-hex head (`gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/compare/<base40>...<head40>`, `…/contents/<path>?ref=<head40>`) or from a private clone whose object store no worker can write; the main checkout's shared `.git/objects` and its `origin/main` ref are not evidence sources, because Codex workers write them (a04 C-80 round 1, 2026-10-19). Evidence files are named and matched by the full 40-hex head, never by a 7-character prefix. Every `gh` invocation names the repository explicitly (`--repo mryfmo/dotfiles` or the full pull-request URL): the local repository configuration selects the default repository otherwise, and a matching head sha does not bind the repository because a fork can hold the same commit (verdict a04 C-80 round 2, 2026-10-19). A mechanism whose application path runs code this contract does not yet cover (the agmsg storage layer sourced by `dispatch.sh`/`history.sh`; the integration gate until its own hardening task lands) names that task as the prerequisite of its `--apply` mode and runs shadow-only until the prerequisite is merged.
111:9. **私設キャッシュ** orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。hook は uv を使わず jq と python 標準ライブラリだけで動く。 One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19). Shell code of other projects (the agmsg storage layer and its `drivers.partition` sourcing) is never sourced by an orchestrator-side mechanism; counters are read from the agmsg SQLite store read-only with a fixed query or from the pasted record.
112:10. **worker は sandbox 外でコマンドを実行しない（D-024-17 の改訂）** 2026-10-19T03:45Z の実測で `gh api` は Claude sandbox の中から成功した（sandbox の proxy は CONNECT 型で TLS を終端しない；keychain のトークンも読めた）。worker 席の除外コマンドは空にし、gh も git push も sandbox 内の proxy 経由で実行する。Codex 席は `sandbox_workspace_write.network_access = true` で同じ状態にし、permgate の codex ポリシーは sandbox 外への escape を承認しない（permgate の変更は操作者の手順）。wrapper 方式（絶対パス、env -i、固定 GH_CONFIG_DIR）は LD_PRELOAD と引数転送（`gh repo clone -- --config`）を閉じられないので採らない。
113:7. **検証** threat_table は本節の項目 1〜10 に 1 行ずつ対応させる（参照だけでは足りない）。共通テスト補助 `tests/unit/trusted_exec_contract.py`（P1-6 で作る）を使い、仕組みごとに 4 つの red→green テストを持つ：PATH 先頭の shim が実行されない、植え付けた GIT_* と git 設定が効かない、worker ルート配下の信頼パスが拒否される、検証後に差し替えたファイルが消費側に届かない。

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# @file ci-rerun.sh
     3	# @brief Rerun a CI run's failed jobs once, and only when every failure is a known flake of the current head.
     4	# @description
     5	#   Plan 024 P1-3 (T133). Reads the run with `gh run view <run-id> --json`, the log of each job
     6	#   whose conclusion is `failure` (`--log-failed`), `timed_out` or `cancelled` (`--log`), and the
     7	#   signatures of `.github/flakes.yaml` (a list of `{id, job, log_regex, note}`; `job` and
     8	#   `log_regex` are extended regular expressions, matched with `grep -E` against the job name and
     9	#   its log). A job with no log matches nothing. A pull-request run must belong to exactly one
    10	#   pull request whose current head is the run's `headSha`, checked before the logs are read and
    11	#   again right before the rerun: a superseded head's rerun would re-enter `pr-<N>` and cancel the
    12	#   newest head's run. When every such job matches a signature, the run is reserved by an atomic
    13	#   mkdir(2) of `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.lock.d/<run-id>` (through python3's os.mkdir), one
    14	#   `<utc>\t<run-id>\t<headSha>\t<job>\t<signature id>` row per job is appended to
    15	#   `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.tsv`, and only then `gh run rerun <run-id> --failed`
    16	#   runs. The reservation stays after success, failure or interruption, so a run is rerun at most
    17	#   once, and it blocks only its own run. All of these live in the checkout this script lives in
    18	#   (`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` there).
    19	# @arg $1 int The workflow run id.
    20	# @exitcode 0 Every candidate job matched a signature; the run was reserved and rerun once.
    21	# @exitcode 1 A gh or jq call or the signature file failed (never gh's own codes), or the reserved rerun failed.
    22	# @exitcode 2 Usage error, a run not completed yet, or a run with no failed, timed-out or cancelled job.
    23	# @exitcode 3 A job matched no signature (`real failure: <job>`) or the head is superseded (`superseded: run <id>`).
    24	# @exitcode 4 The run is already reserved or in ci-reruns.tsv; nothing was rerun.
    25	# @example
    26	#   scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh 39046848147
    27	set -euo pipefail
    28	
    29	if (($# != 1)) || [[ ! $1 =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    30	    echo "usage: ci-rerun.sh <run-id>" >&2
    31	    exit 2
    32	fi
    33	run_id=$1
    34	root="$(git -C "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" rev-parse --show-toplevel)"
    35	flakes="${root}/.github/flakes.yaml"
    36	ledger="${root}/.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.tsv"
    37	reservation="${root}/.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.lock.d/${run_id}"
    38	
    39	if [[ -d ${reservation} ]] ||
    40	    { [[ -f ${ledger} ]] && awk -F '\t' -v id="${run_id}" '$2 == id { found = 1 } END { exit !found }' "${ledger}"; }; then
    41	    echo "already rerun once: run ${run_id} is reserved in ${reservation%/*} or recorded in ${ledger}" >&2
    42	    exit 4
    43	fi

    45	#
    46	# @description Print each signature of the flake file as `id<TAB>job<TAB>log_regex`, refusing a malformed entry.
    47	# @arg $1 string The flake file.
    48	#
    49	function signatures() {
    50	    local script='import sys, yaml
    51	entries = yaml.safe_load(open(sys.argv[1])) or []
    52	if not isinstance(entries, list):
    53	    sys.exit("ci-rerun: " + sys.argv[1] + " is not a list")
    54	for entry in entries:
    55	    fields = [entry.get(key) for key in ("id", "job", "log_regex")] if isinstance(entry, dict) else [None]
    56	    if not all(isinstance(field, str) and field and "\t" not in field and "\n" not in field for field in fields):
    57	        sys.exit("ci-rerun: malformed signature in " + sys.argv[1] + ": " + repr(entry))
    58	    print("\t".join(fields))'
    59	    # The index form fails inside the Claude sandbox even with PyYAML cached, so the cache goes first.
    60	    uv run --offline --no-project --quiet --with pyyaml python -I -c "${script}" "$1" < /dev/null 2> /dev/null ||
    61	        uv run --no-project --quiet --with pyyaml python -I -c "${script}" "$1" < /dev/null
    62	}
    63	
    64	#
    65	# @description Exit 3 when the run's pull request has moved past its head, 1 when that cannot be read.
    66	#
    67	function require_current_head() {
    68	    local prs current
    69	    [[ ${event} == pull_request ]] || return 0
    70	    prs="$(gh api "repos/{owner}/{repo}/actions/runs/${run_id}" --jq '[.pull_requests[].number | tostring] | join(" ")')" || exit 1
    71	    if [[ ! ${prs} =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    72	        echo "ci-rerun: run ${run_id} belongs to no single pull request (${prs:-none})" >&2
    73	        exit 1
    74	    fi
    75	    current="$(gh pr view "${prs}" --json headRefOid --jq .headRefOid)" || exit 1
    76	    [[ ${current} =~ ^[0-9a-f]{40}$ ]] || {
    77	        echo "ci-rerun: pull request ${prs} has no readable head" >&2
    78	        exit 1
    79	    }
    80	    if [[ ${current} != "${head_sha}" ]]; then
    81	        echo "superseded: run ${run_id} ran ${head_sha}, pull request ${prs} is at ${current}"
    82	        exit 3
    83	    fi
    84	}
    85	
    86	# A failed call exits 1: gh's and jq's own codes (gh 4 needs authentication, 2 cancelled) would read as this script's.
    87	run="$(gh run view "${run_id}" --json jobs,status,conclusion,headSha,event)" || exit 1
    88	head_sha="$(jq -r '.headSha // empty' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
    89	event="$(jq -r '.event // empty' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
    90	status="$(jq -r '.status // empty' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
    91	# A run in progress has jobs that are not final yet: --failed would rerun ones no signature checked.
    92	if [[ ${status} != completed ]]; then
    93	    echo "run ${run_id} is not completed (${status:-unknown}); nothing is rerun before it ends" >&2
    94	    exit 2
    95	fi
    96	[[ ${head_sha} =~ ^[0-9a-f]{40}$ ]] || {
    97	    echo "ci-rerun: run ${run_id} has no head sha" >&2
    98	    exit 1
    99	}
   100	candidates="$(jq -r '.jobs[] | select(.conclusion == ("failure", "timed_out", "cancelled"))
   101	    | [.databaseId, .name, .conclusion] | @tsv' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
   102	if [[ -z ${candidates} ]]; then
   103	    echo "run ${run_id} has no failed, timed-out or cancelled job" >&2
   104	    exit 2
   105	fi
   106	require_current_head
   107	known="$(signatures "${flakes}")" || exit 1
   108	
   109	matched=() real=0
   110	while IFS=$'\t' read -r job_id job conclusion; do
   111	    # --log-failed holds only failed steps; a timed-out or cancelled job's log is read whole.
   112	    option=--log-failed
   113	    [[ ${conclusion} == failure ]] || option=--log
   114	    log="$(gh run view "${run_id}" --job "${job_id}" "${option}")" || exit 1
   115	    signature=""
   116	    while [[ -n ${log} ]] && IFS=$'\t' read -r id job_regex log_regex; do
   117	        if grep -Eq -- "${job_regex}" <<< "${job}" && grep -Eq -- "${log_regex}" <<< "${log}"; then
   118	            signature=${id}
   119	            break
   120	        fi
   121	    done <<< "${known}"
   122	    if [[ -z ${signature} ]]; then
   123	        echo "real failure: ${job}"
   124	        real=1
   125	    else
   126	        matched+=("${job}"$'\t'"${signature}")
   127	    fi
   128	done <<< "${candidates}"
   129	((real == 0)) || exit 3
   130	
   131	# Reserve before rerunning: the directory and the rows outlive a rerun that fails, is refused or is interrupted.
   132	# The claim is mkdir(2) itself: uutils' mkdir (Ubuntu 26.04) returned 0 for a directory another call had just created,
   133	# until uutils bf423f388d (2026-08-28), so the mkdir binary cannot decide which of two concurrent calls wins.
   134	mkdir -p "${reservation%/*}"
   135	claim=0
   136	python3 -I -c 'import os, sys
   137	try:
   138	    os.mkdir(sys.argv[1])
   139	except FileExistsError:
   140	    sys.exit(4)' "${reservation}" 2> /dev/null || claim=$?
   141	if ((claim == 4)); then
   142	    echo "already rerun once: run ${run_id} was reserved by a concurrent call" >&2
   143	    exit 4
   144	elif ((claim != 0)); then
   145	    echo "ci-rerun: cannot create ${reservation}" >&2
   146	    exit 1
   147	fi
   148	now="$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"
   149	for row in "${matched[@]}"; do
   150	    printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' "${now}" "${run_id}" "${head_sha}" "${row}" >> "${ledger}"
   151	done
   152	require_current_head
   153	if ! gh run rerun "${run_id}" --failed; then
   154	    echo "ci-rerun: gh run rerun failed; run ${run_id} stays reserved in ${reservation%/*}" >&2
   155	    exit 1
   156	fi
   157	printf 'rerun: run %s, %d job(s), recorded in %s\n' "${run_id}" "${#matched[@]}" "${ledger}"

D-024-23 supersedes D-024-22's credential-separation claim because gh Keychain access is shared by seats running as the same macOS user. Prevention requires a separate OS user or host; the current GitHub boundary is detection, not prevention.

   168	# AGMSG-TASK dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01 — INV-6 reset of a01 on tools/worker-write-boundary-a01, seat a010; plan 024 P1-15 under D-024-17, D-024-19, D-024-20, D-024-21 and D-024-23

   170	## Goal
   171	
   172	This is the amendment-1 text of the task dispatched at 2026-10-19T05:42Z (the ninth-round text that passed its pre-dispatch security review). Amendment 1 folds in two interpretation notes the worker needed (the inherited make format clause; the portable tracked `.claude/settings.json` with host-specific paths only in the rendered guard) and removes the Codex worker profile item (a Codex seat edits no Codex-boundary source; it becomes the separate Claude-seat task dotfiles-T133-codex-worker-network-a01). Everything else is the dispatched text in meaning; the branch, the eight green tests and the fixtures stay; the worker swaps the task copy to this text at its next push.
   173	
   174	The host is not a security boundary between seats sharing one OS user. gh keys Keychain credentials by host and user, so a worker running as the same macOS user can read the orchestrator's token regardless of `GH_CONFIG_DIR`. Prevention of a worker-initiated merge requires a separate OS user or host under D-024-23's future P1-16, which the operator decides whether to pursue. Until then the GitHub boundary is detection, not prevention. The regime detects missing acceptance or gate-passage evidence in the next records PR, not at main-push time, because the records reach main only with that boundary PR. On a PR touching `.orchestration/acceptance/**`, the regime job checks every main merge commit since the previous boundary for its corresponding acceptance record with gate-passage evidence and fails the PR when one is missing. P1-11 owns implementation of that check; this task states section 5.14 item 0's detection rule. Approvals and gate evidence of record are read from GitHub at a SHA through its API inside the sandbox. Host-local controls reduce accidental and injected damage without establishing isolation from a malicious same-UID process.
   175	
   176	This task implements section 5.14 items 1, 9 and 10 through a dedicated Claude worker configuration directory, static Edit deny rules on trusted paths and worktree configuration, managed sandbox enforcement, guarded project hooks, hook interpreter and PATH bindings, private worker caches and the rule that worker commands run inside their sandbox. Every Claude seat has `sandbox.failIfUnavailable: true`.
   177	
   178	The administrative managed policy is shared by every Claude seat. Its Herdr socket allowance and three control-plane exclusions remain available to workers as well as the orchestrator. Empty arrays in worker user settings do not subtract these managed entries. Their availability is a residual under item 0, and the worker execution rule supplies no authorization to use them outside the sandbox.
   179	
   180	[memory:decision] D-024-20 makes this task the dispatch prerequisite of P1-14 a05, P1-10 a02, audit-pool-a02 and P1-6. The orchestrator places this task in their `after:` fields before dispatch. This task has `after: []`. A Codex seat performs these Claude configuration edits under the routing rule, including the Worker Playbook step 4 sentence permitted by plan 021 step 8.3. This Codex seat edits no Codex-boundary source.
   181	
   182	The design source is `.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md` section 5.14 items 0 through 10.
   183	
   184	C-88: The following verbatim design sentences are normative for this task; the path is context only. The worker resolves `$HOME` when reading `$HOME/Workspace/dotfiles/.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md`, and no tracked task text or task copy carries a literal operator home path, as required by the validator's home-path scan.
   185	
   186	> 5.14 item 1: Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。 worker 設定：除外コマンド無し、Herdr socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。 hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。 `failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。
   187	
   188	> 5.14 item 2: 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。
   189	
   190	> 5.14 item 9: orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。 `UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。 hook は uv を使わず jq と python 標準ライブラリだけで動く。

Found one security issue in [PR #424](https://github.com/mryfmo/dotfiles/pull/424), head `44cb835`.

- **P1 · High confidence · `scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh:45` — The fallback still accepts a checkout-controlled interpreter from an activated virtualenv’s absolute PATH entry, leaving the owned interpreter-selection vulnerability open.**

  When `mise` is absent or cannot resolve `python3`, `command -v` can return `<checkout>/.venv/bin/python3`. Changing directory to `/` does not neutralize absolute PATH entries, and line 47 accepts this path because its directory is not named `shims`. The selected executable subsequently runs through uv and directly at line 162 with the operator’s authority; the latter retains the inherited environment, including any credentials.

  Resolve the fallback from explicitly trusted tool locations. Extend the regression test at `tests/unit/test_ci_rerun.py:273` with a marker-writing `python3` in `.venv/bin` **prepended to PATH**, with mise unavailable. The current fixture sets `VIRTUAL_ENV` but omits that activation behavior. This finding is based on code tracing, not an executed exploit.

Threat-table assessment:

| Rows | Assessment |
|---|---|
| Script and tests | Changes fit the owned interpreter-selection work, but the finding prevents closure. |
| Workflow/interpreter/environment inputs | **Insufficient mitigation:** excluding interpreter files from `allowed_files` does not prevent the helper from executing checkout-controlled files supplied through PATH. |
| Lint baseline | Unchanged; no baseline suppression introduced. |
| Task copy, metadata, separators | Local SHA-256 matches the report; correspondence with the original dispatch remains unverified. |
| Evidence records | Treated as untrusted claims; historical review receipts do not establish final-head correctness. |
| In-process effects | Test code installs a failing mise stub and fake gh; no live GitHub action is apparent in the fixtures. |
| New/renamed scripts | None introduced. |
| Merge, update-branch, forced removal | Procedural controls remain the orchestrator’s responsibility; this diff neither implements nor verifies their execution. |

No additional changeset-specific injection, unsafe deserialization, path-traversal, or authentication/authorization issue was identified. YAML continues to use `safe_load`; the earlier pre-pinning `env` and cleanup-command issues are fixed.

📝 まとめ: Pinned-head security review completed with one P1 finding; acceptance remains with the orchestrator.
Not checked: runtime tests, live CI, or dispatch provenance; filesystem access was read-only and the gh-first API request failed. The remaining risk is checkout-controlled execution with operator authority.

Review comment:

- [P1] Use an absolute path for cleanup before PATH is pinned — scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh:42-42
  When invoked from a PR checkout with an empty or `.` PATH entry, a missing or rejected tool causes the loop to exit before PATH is pinned. This trap then resolves `rm` through the inherited PATH, executing a checkout-supplied executable with the operator's privileges and credential access instead of failing closed. Use `/bin/rm` in the trap and extend the planted-tools test to cover tool-resolution failure.

Seat: a011 (Claude, worker-d), holding. Advisory owner: dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a03.

- 2026-10-19T08:27Z: text installed (198 lines); `task-consistency-check.py: OK`; no literal home path; C-80 review started.
- 2026-10-19T08:30:49Z: C-80 first review (`…-text-security-*.md`, newest): `Verdict: incorrect`: P1 on Linux an inherited LD_PRELOAD/LD_AUDIT executes while the loader maps /bin/bash, before `-p` or the first statement, so the self re-exec is not a boundary (the trusted parent must supply the clean environment); P1 deriving the install root from HOME lets a forged HOME redirect interpreter selection (anchor to the passwd home of the real uid); P1 (medium) re-executing `"$0"` reopens the caller's pathname (symlink swap between executions); P2 the parser row's closure test is a PATH test; P2 the item 3 row's test cannot close the broader trusted-path boundary and omits case aliases. Root cause (design tier): 5.14 item 2 let the program sanitize itself by re-exec; the boundary is the trusted parent's `env -i`, and the program can only verify and refuse. Design 5.14 items 2 and 9 amended (verify-and-refuse; `$0` realpath; HOME = passwd home; install root anchored to it). Decision: round 2 by the review-profile context (prompt `ce-a04r2-redesign-prompt.md`); the same amendment reaches the verdict a04 text at its next round.

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# @file ci-rerun.sh
     3	# @brief Rerun a CI run's failed jobs once, and only when every failure is a known flake of the current head.
     4	# @description
     5	#   Plan 024 P1-3 (T133). Reads the run with `gh run view <run-id> --json`, the log of each job
     6	#   whose conclusion is `failure` (`--log-failed`), `timed_out` or `cancelled` (`--log`), and the
     7	#   signatures of `.github/flakes.yaml` (a list of `{id, job, log_regex, note}`; `job` and
     8	#   `log_regex` are extended regular expressions, matched with `grep -E` against the job name and
     9	#   its log). A job with no log matches nothing. A pull-request run must belong to exactly one
    10	#   pull request whose current head is the run's `headSha`, checked before the logs are read and
    11	#   again right before the rerun: a superseded head's rerun would re-enter `pr-<N>` and cancel the
    12	#   newest head's run. When every such job matches a signature, the run is reserved by an atomic
    13	#   mkdir(2) of `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.lock.d/<run-id>` (through python3's os.mkdir), one
    14	#   `<utc>\t<run-id>\t<headSha>\t<job>\t<signature id>` row per job is appended to
    15	#   `.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.tsv`, and only then `gh run rerun <run-id> --failed`
    16	#   runs. The reservation stays after success, failure or interruption, so a run is rerun at most
    17	#   once, and it blocks only its own run. All of these live in the checkout this script lives in
    18	#   (`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` there).
    19	# @arg $1 int The workflow run id.
    20	# @exitcode 0 Every candidate job matched a signature; the run was reserved and rerun once.
    21	# @exitcode 1 A gh or jq call or the signature file failed (never gh's own codes), or the reserved rerun failed.
    22	# @exitcode 2 Usage error, a run not completed yet, or a run with no failed, timed-out or cancelled job.
    23	# @exitcode 3 A job matched no signature (`real failure: <job>`) or the head is superseded (`superseded: run <id>`).
    24	# @exitcode 4 The run is already reserved or in ci-reruns.tsv; nothing was rerun.
    25	# @example
    26	#   scripts/orchestrator/ci-rerun.sh 39046848147
    27	set -euo pipefail
    28	
    29	if (($# != 1)) || [[ ! $1 =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    30	    echo "usage: ci-rerun.sh <run-id>" >&2
    31	    exit 2
    32	fi
    33	run_id=$1
    34	root="$(git -C "$(dirname -- "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" rev-parse --show-toplevel)"
    35	flakes="${root}/.github/flakes.yaml"
    36	ledger="${root}/.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.tsv"
    37	reservation="${root}/.orchestration/validation/ci-reruns.lock.d/${run_id}"
    38	
    39	if [[ -d ${reservation} ]] ||
    40	    { [[ -f ${ledger} ]] && awk -F '\t' -v id="${run_id}" '$2 == id { found = 1 } END { exit !found }' "${ledger}"; }; then
    41	    echo "already rerun once: run ${run_id} is reserved in ${reservation%/*} or recorded in ${ledger}" >&2
    42	    exit 4
    43	fi
    44	
    45	#
    46	# @description Print each signature of the flake file as `id<TAB>job<TAB>log_regex`, refusing a malformed entry.
    47	# @arg $1 string The flake file.
    48	#
    49	function signatures() {
    50	    local script='import sys, yaml
    51	entries = yaml.safe_load(open(sys.argv[1])) or []
    52	if not isinstance(entries, list):
    53	    sys.exit("ci-rerun: " + sys.argv[1] + " is not a list")
    54	for entry in entries:
    55	    fields = [entry.get(key) for key in ("id", "job", "log_regex")] if isinstance(entry, dict) else [None]
    56	    if not all(isinstance(field, str) and field and "\t" not in field and "\n" not in field for field in fields):
    57	        sys.exit("ci-rerun: malformed signature in " + sys.argv[1] + ": " + repr(entry))
    58	    print("\t".join(fields))'
    59	    # The index form fails inside the Claude sandbox even with PyYAML cached, so the cache goes first.
    60	    uv run --offline --no-project --quiet --with pyyaml python -I -c "${script}" "$1" < /dev/null 2> /dev/null ||
    61	        uv run --no-project --quiet --with pyyaml python -I -c "${script}" "$1" < /dev/null
    62	}
    63	
    64	#
    65	# @description Exit 3 when the run's pull request has moved past its head, 1 when that cannot be read.
    66	#
    67	function require_current_head() {
    68	    local prs current
    69	    [[ ${event} == pull_request ]] || return 0
    70	    prs="$(gh api "repos/{owner}/{repo}/actions/runs/${run_id}" --jq '[.pull_requests[].number | tostring] | join(" ")')" || exit 1
    71	    if [[ ! ${prs} =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    72	        echo "ci-rerun: run ${run_id} belongs to no single pull request (${prs:-none})" >&2
    73	        exit 1
    74	    fi
    75	    current="$(gh pr view "${prs}" --json headRefOid --jq .headRefOid)" || exit 1
    76	    [[ ${current} =~ ^[0-9a-f]{40}$ ]] || {
    77	        echo "ci-rerun: pull request ${prs} has no readable head" >&2
    78	        exit 1
    79	    }
    80	    if [[ ${current} != "${head_sha}" ]]; then
    81	        echo "superseded: run ${run_id} ran ${head_sha}, pull request ${prs} is at ${current}"
    82	        exit 3
    83	    fi
    84	}
    85	
    86	# A failed call exits 1: gh's and jq's own codes (gh 4 needs authentication, 2 cancelled) would read as this script's.
    87	run="$(gh run view "${run_id}" --json jobs,status,conclusion,headSha,event)" || exit 1
    88	head_sha="$(jq -r '.headSha // empty' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
    89	event="$(jq -r '.event // empty' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
    90	status="$(jq -r '.status // empty' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
    91	# A run in progress has jobs that are not final yet: --failed would rerun ones no signature checked.
    92	if [[ ${status} != completed ]]; then
    93	    echo "run ${run_id} is not completed (${status:-unknown}); nothing is rerun before it ends" >&2
    94	    exit 2
    95	fi
    96	[[ ${head_sha} =~ ^[0-9a-f]{40}$ ]] || {
    97	    echo "ci-rerun: run ${run_id} has no head sha" >&2
    98	    exit 1
    99	}
   100	candidates="$(jq -r '.jobs[] | select(.conclusion == ("failure", "timed_out", "cancelled"))
   101	    | [.databaseId, .name, .conclusion] | @tsv' <<< "${run}")" || exit 1
   102	if [[ -z ${candidates} ]]; then
   103	    echo "run ${run_id} has no failed, timed-out or cancelled job" >&2
   104	    exit 2
   105	fi

   107	known="$(signatures "${flakes}")" || exit 1
   108	
   109	matched=() real=0
   110	while IFS=$'\t' read -r job_id job conclusion; do
   111	    # --log-failed holds only failed steps; a timed-out or cancelled job's log is read whole.
   112	    option=--log-failed
   113	    [[ ${conclusion} == failure ]] || option=--log
   114	    log="$(gh run view "${run_id}" --job "${job_id}" "${option}")" || exit 1

   120	        fi

   122	    if [[ -z ${signature} ]]; then
   123	        echo "real failure: ${job}"
   124	        real=1
   125	    else
   126	        matched+=("${job}"$'\t'"${signature}")
   127	    fi
   128	done <<< "${candidates}"
   129	((real == 0)) || exit 3
   130	
   131	# Reserve before rerunning: the directory and the rows outlive a rerun that fails, is refused or is interrupted.
   132	# The claim is mkdir(2) itself: uutils' mkdir (Ubuntu 26.04) returned 0 for a directory another call had just created,
   133	# until uutils bf423f388d (2026-08-28), so the mkdir binary cannot decide which of two concurrent calls wins.
   134	mkdir -p "${reservation%/*}"
   135	claim=0
   136	python3 -I -c 'import os, sys
   137	try:
   138	    os.mkdir(sys.argv[1])
   139	except FileExistsError:
   140	    sys.exit(4)' "${reservation}" 2> /dev/null || claim=$?
   141	if ((claim == 4)); then
   142	    echo "already rerun once: run ${run_id} was reserved by a concurrent call" >&2
   143	    exit 4
   144	elif ((claim != 0)); then
   145	    echo "ci-rerun: cannot create ${reservation}" >&2
   146	    exit 1
   147	fi
   148	now="$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"
   149	for row in "${matched[@]}"; do
   150	    printf '%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n' "${now}" "${run_id}" "${head_sha}" "${row}" >> "${ledger}"
   151	done
   152	require_current_head
   153	if ! gh run rerun "${run_id}" --failed; then
   154	    echo "ci-rerun: gh run rerun failed; run ${run_id} stays reserved in ${reservation%/*}" >&2
   155	    exit 1
   156	fi
   157	printf 'rerun: run %s, %d job(s), recorded in %s\n' "${run_id}" "${#matched[@]}" "${ledger}"

以外）、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.claude/hooks`、信頼ルートのバイナリ、orchestrator の証跡・スナップショット・lock は worker から書けない。render テストが描画後の deny を検証する。残余は「どの席 sandbox にも属さない同一 UID プロセス」と、Worker Playbook step 4 が sandbox 外に出す少数のコマンド（gh）。後者は D-024-17（P1-15）で絶対パスの実行体に束縛する。
104-2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
105-3. **信頼パスの境界検査** 仕組みが信頼するパス（スクリプト、台帳、証跡、worktree、lock、出力先）は realpath で解決し、`.claude/worktrees/` または manifest の worker ルート配下なら拒否（ask/hold）。symlink 構成要素は解決後に比較し、大小文字を区別しないファイルシステムでは同一性比較を大小文字非依存で行う。
106-4. **検証したものを実行し、検証した内容を渡す** 検証した実行体はその realpath で実行する（再探索しない）。検証したファイル内容は信頼ルート配下のスナップショット（0600、一意名）として渡し、使用直前に sha256 を再照合する。判断に読んだ証跡は sha256 を出力に記録する。
107-5. **出力と予約の同一性** 名前には 40 桁の head を使う（7 桁で衝突させない）。lock は信頼ルート配下の固定 inode に O_EXCL で作り、置換を inode 比較で検出する。物理スロット 1 つに lock 1 つ。
108-6. **正直な残余** 境界だけが緩和するリスクを `closed` と書かない。各文は残余を 1 行で明記する。
109-0. **脅威モデル（D-024-21）** 席は同じ OS ユーザで動き、ホストは席間の信頼境界ではない（同一 UID のプロセスは symlink 差し替え、共有 `.git` の object 差し替え、hook の子プロセス、Herdr socket 経由の host 実行で、ホスト内のどの検査も迂回できる）。regime の硬い境界は GitHub（保護された main、必須チェック、orchestrator の identity によるマージ）。ただしこの境界は **worker 席が orchestrator の GitHub 資格情報を共有しない**ときだけ成立する（D-024-22：worker 席は専用の GitHub identity（別アカウントまたは App のトークン）で `gh` に login し、ruleset の bypass 一覧に入らない。orchestrator の keychain トークンを worker の config dir に持ち込まない。identity が分かれるまで「GitHub が境界」は未成立と書く）。**同一 macOS ユーザでは資格情報は分離できない**（gh は keychain の項目を host/user で引き、同じ UID の gh は orchestrator のトークンを読める。`GH_CONFIG_DIR` は設定を分けるだけ）。防止には worker 席を別の OS ユーザ（または別ホスト）で動かすしかなく、それは D-024-23 の将来項目（P1-16、操作者の判断）。それまで GitHub 境界は **検知**で守る：記録は boundary の記録 PR まで main に無いので、検知は次の記録 PR の regime ジョブで行う（`.orchestration/acceptance/**` に触れる PR で、前回 boundary 以降の main の各マージ commit に、gate 通過の証跡を持つ受け入れ記録が対応することを検査し、無ければその PR を失敗させる；P1-11 owner a02 の行 `ci:detection:1`）。main push 時点の検知は記録が無いので不可能であり、文は「次の記録 PR で検知、防止ではない」と書く。悪意ある worker に耐えるべき入力（承認台帳、gate が読む証跡 of record）は GitHub から sha 指定で API 経由（sandbox 内）に読み、ローカルファイルやローカル object database からは読まない。ホスト内の制御（本節 1〜10）は事故と prompt injection による損害の抑制であり、文はそう書く。
110-8. **承認はコミット済み台帳から読む** permission の決定行（`scope=permission approved_by=user diff_sha256=…`）は anchored checkout の作業コピーではなく、コミット済みの台帳から読む。読み方は `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` が返す commit sha を取り、GitHub API（`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/contents/.orchestration/decisions.tsv?ref=<sha>`、sandbox 内）でその sha の内容を取得する。ローカルの `git show` は共有 object database が書けるため中間 tree の差し替えで偽物を返しうる（第 3 回審査の所見 2）（ローカルの ref 名 `origin/main` は経由しない。`refs/replace/` も書けるので replacement を無効にする。Codex worker は `.git/objects` と `.git/refs` に書けるので ref は差し替えられるが、sha で指した object は内容アドレスで偽造できない）。作業コピーの行は提案で、記録 PR が main にマージされて初めて承認になる（PR の承認は操作者の操作）。偽造には main へのマージが要る。 The same applies to the PR content a mechanism evaluates (the verdict, the audit precheck, the counters): the changeset is read from GitHub at the 40-hex head (`gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/compare/<base40>...<head40>`, `…/contents/<path>?ref=<head40>`) or from a private clone whose object store no worker can write; the main checkout's shared `.git/objects` and its `origin/main` ref are not evidence sources, because Codex workers write them (a04 C-80 round 1, 2026-10-19). Evidence files are named and matched by the full 40-hex head, never by a 7-character prefix. Every `gh` invocation names the repository explicitly (`--repo mryfmo/dotfiles` or the full pull-request URL): the local repository configuration selects the default repository otherwise, and a matching head sha does not bind the repository because a fork can hold the same commit (verdict a04 C-80 round 2, 2026-10-19). A mechanism whose application path runs code this contract does not yet cover (the agmsg storage layer sourced by `dispatch.sh`/`history.sh`; the integration gate until its own hardening task lands) names that task as the prerequisite of its `--apply` mode and runs shadow-only until the prerequisite is merged.
111-9. **私設キャッシュ** orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。hook は uv を使わず jq と python 標準ライブラリだけで動く。 One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19). Shell code of other projects (the agmsg storage layer and its `drivers.partition` sourcing) is never sourced by an orchestrator-side mechanism; counters are read from the agmsg SQLite store read-only with a fixed query or from the pasted record.
112-10. **worker は sandbox 外でコマンドを実行しない（D-024-17 の改訂）** 2026-10-19T03:45Z の実測で `gh api` は Claude sandbox の中から成功した（sandbox の proxy は CONNECT 型で TLS を終端しない；keychain のトークンも読めた）。worker 席の除外コマンドは空にし、gh も git push も sandbox 内の proxy 経由で実行する。Codex 席は `sandbox_workspace_write.network_access = true` で同じ状態にし、permgate の codex ポリシーは sandbox 外への escape を承認しない（permgate の変更は操作者の手順）。wrapper 方式（絶対パス、env -i、固定 GH_CONFIG_DIR）は LD_PRELOAD と引数転送（`gh repo clone -- --config`）を閉じられないので採らない。
113-7. **検証** threat_table は本節の項目 1〜10 に 1 行ずつ対応させる（参照だけでは足りない）。共通テスト補助 `tests/unit/trusted_exec_contract.py`（P1-6 で作る）を使い、仕組みごとに 4 つの red→green テストを持つ：PATH 先頭の shim が実行されない、植え付けた GIT_* と git 設定が効かない、worker ルート配下の信頼パスが拒否される、検証後に差し替えたファイルが消費側に届かない。

25:- 2026-10-19T03:58:10Z: C-80 third review of the P1-15 text (`…-text-security-ecc7ab5.md`): `Verdict: incorrect`, five P1: (1) a sandboxed worker can swap an allowed directory for a symlink after the hook validated, so the unsandboxed file tool overwrites a trusted path (the race is an escape, not a self-harm); (2) the writable object database defeats sha pinning: a replaced intermediate tree object redirects `git show <sha>:path` to an attacker blob (tree traversal does not re-hash intermediate objects); (3) hook descendants still resolve `python3` and `env bash` through shims (herdr-agent-state.sh, delivery scripts); (4) the Herdr unix socket in the worker sandbox lets a sandboxed client request host execution (`pane run`) through the server; (5) a hook timeout or kill is fail-open unless the per-hook `onFailure: "block"` (Claude Code ≥ 2.1.295) is set.
26:- design conclusion (03:5xZ, pending two fact checks): three text rounds show the host is not a security boundary between seats that share one OS user; the regime's hard boundary is GitHub (protected main, required checks, the merge by the orchestrator's identity), and inputs that must resist a malicious worker (the approval ledger, the gate's evidence of record) are read from GitHub at a sha through the API inside the sandbox, never from local files or the local object database; host-local controls target accidental and injected damage and are stated as such. The per-seat mechanism that makes worker-specific policy possible at all is a separate `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` for worker seats (own settings: no excluded commands, no Herdr socket, minimal trusted hooks with absolute interpreters and `onFailure: block`, Edit denies on trusted paths, failIfUnavailable true); the facts (credentials with a second config dir; onFailure; sandbox per config dir; symlink resolution of file-tool paths) are being verified against the documentation.

   101	orchestrator 側の仕組み（merge gate、integrate-merge、orch-gate、dispositions、audit-pool、verdict、chain）は、同じホスト上で同じ UID の worker プロセスと共存する。2026-10-19 の C-80 審査（a05、dispositions-a02、audit-pool-a01 の 3 文、計 19 件の P1）は、どの文も同じ前提を書かず同じ穴を持つことを示した。以後、`security: true` の orchestrator 側タスク文は本節を参照で適用し、本節の各項目に対応する threat 行を持つ。文ごとに言い換えない。

socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。chezmoi は user ではこのファイルを書けないので、描画した内容を操作者が `sudo` で一度配置する（操作者手順）。worker 設定は worktree 自身の `.claude/**` と `.mcp.json` への Edit deny（`//<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**`）を持つ（Bash 側は sandbox 既定の settings/hooks の denyWithinAllow）。agmsg store（全席が書く）から配送 hook が driver や plugin を source する上流の経路（storage.sh の `drivers.partition` traversal、`db/trusted-plugins` と `plugins/partition/shared.sh` による差し替え承認）は別タスク（agmsg hardening）の行で、worker 設定は `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` と `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins` への書き込みを deny し（Edit deny は sandbox の deny にも合流する）、hook 側は store 由来の設定値を検証するまで残余と書く。managed settings の配置先は OS で異なる（macOS `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、Linux `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`）。hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。公式文書：linked worktree の session は **main checkout の `.claude/settings.local.json` を読む**ので、worker 向けの hook を worktree の settings.local.json に書いても読まれず、逆に orchestrator の local hooks が worker session でも走る。したがって orchestrator 側の local hooks も guard 経由にし、worker 側の hook は worker config dir の settings に置く。Edit deny の一覧は `git ls-tree` ではなく描画時の静的一覧（manifest に列挙、`.agents/worklog/**` と `.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**` を含む）とし、`~/.agents/**` の全面 deny は agmsg store の書き込みと矛盾するので、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`、`~/.agents/model-profiles.env` などストア以外を列挙する。公式文書で確認した事実（2026-10-19）：`Edit(path)` の deny は Write/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit にも効き、deny → ask → allow の順で最初の一致が決まる；ただし Read/Edit の deny は **Bash sandbox の設定にも合流する**（"Paths and domains from both sandbox settings and permission rules are merged into the final sandbox configuration"）ので、ユーザー設定（全席共有）に `.orchestration/**` の Edit deny を書くと orchestrator の Bash による記録書き込みも止まる。hook は起動時に固定されず、設定ファイルの編集は file watcher が即座に反映する。`sandbox.failIfUnavailable` の既定は fail-open（依存が無ければ sandbox 無しで実行）。したがって Claude 席の境界は Edit deny 規則ではなく、**ユーザーレベルの PreToolUse hook**（`~/.claude/hooks/worker-write-boundary`、chezmoi が描画、`/bin/sh` または絶対パスの `python3 -I`）で作る：cwd の realpath が `<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/` 配下の session では、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit の対象が cwd の外、または `<cwd>/.claude/**`・`<cwd>/.mcp.json` のとき `permissionDecision: deny` を返す。Bash 側は sandbox の既定 `denyWithinAllow`（settings、hooks）と `allowWrite`（自席 worktree）が担う。`failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。 Codex 席は sandbox（workspace-write）がツールの書き込み全部を自席の worktree と列挙パスに限る。Claude 席は Seatbelt sandbox が Bash を限るだけで、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit、hook、MCP プロセスは sandbox 外で動き、permission policy（permgate の決定論ポリシー）が path で拒否しない限り anchored checkout や `~/.agents` にも書ける（2026-10-19 時点、描画済み設定にその deny は無い）。したがって前提 6 は「Codex 席は成立、Claude 席は P1-15 が描画設定に Edit の path deny を入れるまで残余」と書く。P1-15 以前に「worker は信頼ルートに書けない」と書く文は誤りで、C-80 が拒否する。P1-15 が入るまで、承認の出所は項目 8 の「コミット済み台帳」で守る。書き込みは自席の worktree（`.claude/worktrees/<name>`）、scratch、列挙されたツールキャッシュに限る。anchored checkout（`.claude/worktrees/` 以外）、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.claude/hooks`、信頼ルートのバイナリ、orchestrator の証跡・スナップショット・lock は worker から書けない。render テストが描画後の deny を検証する。残余は「どの席 sandbox にも属さない同一 UID プロセス」と、Worker Playbook step 4 が sandbox 外に出す少数のコマンド（gh）。後者は D-024-17（P1-15）で絶対パスの実行体に束縛する。
   104	2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
   105	3. **信頼パスの境界検査** 仕組みが信頼するパス（スクリプト、台帳、証跡、worktree、lock、出力先）は realpath で解決し、`.claude/worktrees/` または manifest の worker ルート配下なら拒否（ask/hold）。symlink 構成要素は解決後に比較し、大小文字を区別しないファイルシステムでは同一性比較を大小文字非依存で行う。
   106	4. **検証したものを実行し、検証した内容を渡す** 検証した実行体はその realpath で実行する（再探索しない）。検証したファイル内容は信頼ルート配下のスナップショット（0600、一意名）として渡し、使用直前に sha256 を再照合する。判断に読んだ証跡は sha256 を出力に記録する。
   107	5. **出力と予約の同一性** 名前には 40 桁の head を使う（7 桁で衝突させない）。lock は信頼ルート配下の固定 inode に O_EXCL で作り、置換を inode 比較で検出する。物理スロット 1 つに lock 1 つ。
   108	6. **正直な残余** 境界だけが緩和するリスクを `closed` と書かない。各文は残余を 1 行で明記する。
   109	0. **脅威モデル（D-024-21）** 席は同じ OS ユーザで動き、ホストは席間の信頼境界ではない（同一 UID のプロセスは symlink 差し替え、共有 `.git` の object 差し替え、hook の子プロセス、Herdr socket 経由の host 実行で、ホスト内のどの検査も迂回できる）。regime の硬い境界は GitHub（保護された main、必須チェック、orchestrator の identity によるマージ）。ただしこの境界は **worker 席が orchestrator の GitHub 資格情報を共有しない**ときだけ成立する（D-024-22：worker 席は専用の GitHub identity（別アカウントまたは App のトークン）で `gh` に login し、ruleset の bypass 一覧に入らない。orchestrator の keychain トークンを worker の config dir に持ち込まない。identity が分かれるまで「GitHub が境界」は未成立と書く）。**同一 macOS ユーザでは資格情報は分離できない**（gh は keychain の項目を host/user で引き、同じ UID の gh は orchestrator のトークンを読める。`GH_CONFIG_DIR` は設定を分けるだけ）。防止には worker 席を別の OS ユーザ（または別ホスト）で動かすしかなく、それは D-024-23 の将来項目（P1-16、操作者の判断）。それまで GitHub 境界は **検知**で守る：記録は boundary の記録 PR まで main に無いので、検知は次の記録 PR の regime ジョブで行う（`.orchestration/acceptance/**` に触れる PR で、前回 boundary 以降の main の各マージ commit に、gate 通過の証跡を持つ受け入れ記録が対応することを検査し、無ければその PR を失敗させる；P1-11 owner a02 の行 `ci:detection:1`）。main push 時点の検知は記録が無いので不可能であり、文は「次の記録 PR で検知、防止ではない」と書く。悪意ある worker に耐えるべき入力（承認台帳、gate が読む証跡 of record）は GitHub から sha 指定で API 経由（sandbox 内）に読み、ローカルファイルやローカル object database からは読まない。ホスト内の制御（本節 1〜10）は事故と prompt injection による損害の抑制であり、文はそう書く。
   110	8. **承認はコミット済み台帳から読む** permission の決定行（`scope=permission approved_by=user diff_sha256=…`）は anchored checkout の作業コピーではなく、コミット済みの台帳から読む。読み方は `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` が返す commit sha を取り、GitHub API（`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/contents/.orchestration/decisions.tsv?ref=<sha>`、sandbox 内）でその sha の内容を取得する。ローカルの `git show` は共有 object database が書けるため中間 tree の差し替えで偽物を返しうる（第 3 回審査の所見 2）（ローカルの ref 名 `origin/main` は経由しない。`refs/replace/` も書けるので replacement を無効にする。Codex worker は `.git/objects` と `.git/refs` に書けるので ref は差し替えられるが、sha で指した object は内容アドレスで偽造できない）。作業コピーの行は提案で、記録 PR が main にマージされて初めて承認になる（PR の承認は操作者の操作）。偽造には main へのマージが要る。 The same applies to the PR content a mechanism evaluates (the verdict, the audit precheck, the counters): the changeset is read from GitHub at the 40-hex head (`gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/compare/<base40>...<head40>`, `…/contents/<path>?ref=<head40>`) or from a private clone whose object store no worker can write; the main checkout's shared `.git/objects` and its `origin/main` ref are not evidence sources, because Codex workers write them (a04 C-80 round 1, 2026-10-19). Evidence files are named and matched by the full 40-hex head, never by a 7-character prefix. Every `gh` invocation names the repository explicitly (`--repo mryfmo/dotfiles` or the full pull-request URL): the local repository configuration selects the default repository otherwise, and a matching head sha does not bind the repository because a fork can hold the same commit (verdict a04 C-80 round 2, 2026-10-19). A mechanism whose application path runs code this contract does not yet cover (the agmsg storage layer sourced by `dispatch.sh`/`history.sh`; the integration gate until its own hardening task lands) names that task as the prerequisite of its `--apply` mode and runs shadow-only until the prerequisite is merged.
   111	9. **私設キャッシュ** orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。hook は uv を使わず jq と python 標準ライブラリだけで動く。 One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19). Shell code of other projects (the agmsg storage layer and its `drivers.partition` sourcing) is never sourced by an orchestrator-side mechanism; counters are read from the agmsg SQLite store read-only with a fixed query or from the pasted record.
   112	10. **worker は sandbox 外でコマンドを実行しない（D-024-17 の改訂）** 2026-10-19T03:45Z の実測で `gh api` は Claude sandbox の中から成功した（sandbox の proxy は CONNECT 型で TLS を終端しない；keychain のトークンも読めた）。worker 席の除外コマンドは空にし、gh も git push も sandbox 内の proxy 経由で実行する。Codex 席は `sandbox_workspace_write.network_access = true` で同じ状態にし、permgate の codex ポリシーは sandbox 外への escape を承認しない（permgate の変更は操作者の手順）。wrapper 方式（絶対パス、env -i、固定 GH_CONFIG_DIR）は LD_PRELOAD と引数転送（`gh repo clone -- --config`）を閉じられないので採らない。
   113	7. **検証** threat_table は本節の項目 1〜10 に 1 行ずつ対応させる（参照だけでは足りない）。共通テスト補助 `tests/unit/trusted_exec_contract.py`（P1-6 で作る）を使い、仕組みごとに 4 つの red→green テストを持つ：PATH 先頭の shim が実行されない、植え付けた GIT_* と git 設定が効かない、worker ルート配下の信頼パスが拒否される、検証後に差し替えたファイルが消費側に届かない。
  "pr": null,
  "searches": [],
  "task": "dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a02"
}

**Analyzing environment and interpreter behaviors**

[Loader documentation](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man8/ld.so.8.html).
- [P1] High confidence .orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:107 — Preserving caller-supplied `HOME` and deriving the trusted installation from it lets a counterfeit HOME redirect interpreter selection; deployment checks of the genuine installation do not bind this runtime input—anchor the installation root independently and test forged HOME.
- [P1] Medium confidence .orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:95 — Re-executing `"$0"` before path validation reopens the caller’s pathname: a sandboxed worker can replace a worker-owned symlink to the trusted script between initial execution and re-entry, selecting different code without replacing any protected installation.
- [P2] High confidence .orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:25 — The named closure test exercises hostile PATH selection, not the row’s declaration-parser contract; it cannot establish rejection of duplicate, malformed, non-exact or metacharacter-bearing declarations.
- [P2] High confidence .orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T133-ci-efficiency-followup-a04.md:51 — The interpreter-only refusal test cannot close item 3’s broader trusted-path boundary for scripts, declaration files, cache, ledger and reservation destinations; the specified cases also omit case-alias coverage.

1:Two security findings for [PR #409](https://github.com/mryfmo/dotfiles/pull/409), head `4fe243803971bd5ba8ab45f8ede5cfb4dd37f719`.
6:2. **P2 · High confidence · `.github/workflows/remote.yaml:78` — Quoted filenames bypass the new bootstrap relevance filter.**  
7:   A PR adding `home/run_before_probe".sh` produces a Git pathname beginning with `"`, so `^home/` does not match and all public-bootstrap jobs report success without bootstrapping. Git escapes quotes regardless of `core.quotePath=false`. I verified that the production regex rejects this representation while the base predicate runs it. The unit-test relevance predicate also misses it, leaving applied code without either check. Use NUL-delimited paths and add unusual-filename cases. [Git’s quoting rules](https://git-scm.com/docs/git-config#Documentation/git-config.txt-corequotePath).
